# Phase 5 Loop Dashboard

Resumption pointer for the /loop driver. Cheaper to read than STATUS.md (which is the authoritative append-log).

## Current state

HEAD at `J352` — **Post-J342 service-port arc, slice 10 (Arc 3 slice 6 — DRAINS Arc 3 + COMPLETES the Post-J342 service-port arc, B-category): Queue v5 includeSubresources[] enum-array gating + Episodes-plural hydration**. The final slice — closes Arc 3 and all three service-port arcs. C# v5 `QueueController.cs:170-171` derives `includeSeries = includeSubresources.Contains(QueueSubresource.Series)` + `includeEpisodes = includeSubresources.Contains(QueueSubresource.Episodes)` from the `includeSubresources QueueSubresource[]` query param (enum {Series, Episodes}), and `QueueResource.cs:59` sets `Episodes = includeEpisodes ? model.Episodes?.ToResource() : null`. Three changes: (1) the shared `projectQueueResource` now hydrates the plural `Episodes *[]EpisodeResource` from all q.Episodes (alongside the J348 Series + singular Episode); (2) the v3 `QueueHandler` strips the plural Episodes unconditionally (v3's wire carries only the singular Episode — the plural is a v5-only field the shared adapter hydrates); (3) a new `apiv3.QueueHandlerV5` (lives in apiv3 alongside the shared QueueResource + the unexported parsePagingRequest) parses `includeSubresources` (repeated, case-insensitive "series"/"episodes"), keeps Series iff ∋series, keeps plural Episodes iff ∋episodes, and strips the singular Episode unconditionally (the v5 QueueResource dropped it). `queuev5.ListHandler` switches from the v3 alias to `apiv3.QueueHandlerV5`. The J347 scalar adapter test's Episodes-nil guard was updated to assert the plural is now hydrated. 1 new v5 handler test (`TestListHandler_IncludeSubresources_J352`, 4 sub-tests: no-subresources strips all; series-only; episodes-only keeps plural + strips singular; both). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/queue/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (v3 1.441s, v5/queue 1.802s, cmd/sonarr 3.683s); `go vet` + gofmt clean. **Post-J342 service-port arc COMPLETE** — Arc 1 (Release POST searchInfo fallback, J343) + Arc 2 (Parse + ManualImport full hydration, J344-J346) + Arc 3 (Queue paged surface fully populated v3+v5, J347-J352) all drained. The Queue paged list now serves fully-hydrated rows on both surfaces: v3 (scalar + Series/Episode-singular gated by includeSeries/includeEpisode + CustomFormats + Score + StatusMessages) and v5 (the same scalars + EpisodeIDs/SeasonNumbers/EpisodesWithFilesCount + Series/Episodes-plural gated by includeSubresources[]). The next loop tick should emit the arc-complete sentinel and stop (no further service-port arcs remain in the priority list).

HEAD at `J351` — **Post-J342 service-port arc, slice 9 (Arc 3 slice 5, B-category): Queue v5 always-set list fields — EpisodeIDs + SeasonNumbers + EpisodesWithFilesCount projection**. Projects the three ungated v5-additive QueueResource fields. C# v5 `QueueResource.cs:56-57` sets `EpisodeIds = model.Episodes?.Select(e => e.Id).ToList() ?? []` + `SeasonNumbers = model.SeasonNumber.HasValue ? [model.SeasonNumber.Value] : []` (both always), and `:81` sets `EpisodesWithFilesCount = model.Episodes?.Count(e => e.HasFile) ?? 0`. `projectQueueResource` now builds EpisodeIDs from all q.Episodes IDs (always non-nil pointer → mirrors C# `= []`; empty → wire `[]`), SeasonNumbers from q.SeasonNumber (`&[]int{*sn}` when set, else `&[]int{}`), and EpisodesWithFilesCount by counting episodes whose `EpisodeFileID != nil && > 0` (the Go-port equivalent of C# `Episode.HasFile`, which is `EpisodeFileId > 0`). These are v5-additive fields on the shared apiv3 type — v3 clients ignore the extra keys (the established J324/J318 additive-divergence posture); EpisodesWithFilesCount keeps omitempty so 0 collapses to absent (the J318 accepted trade-off). 2 new adapter tests (populated: EpisodeIDs=[11,12] + SeasonNumbers=[3] + EpisodesWithFilesCount=1 with one EpisodeFileID-bearing episode; empty: non-nil len-0 lists matching C# `= []` + count 0). HEAD at `J349` — **Post-J342 service-port arc, slice 7 (Arc 3 slice 3, B-category): Queue CustomFormats list field + CustomFormats/CustomFormatScore projection from the row's RemoteEpisode Subject**. Discovered the Go-port `apiv3.QueueResource` was MISSING the `CustomFormats []CustomFormatResource` list field entirely (the J318 doc-comment listed it as DEFERRED — only `CustomFormatScore int` had been pinned). C# `QueueResource.cs:26` declares `List<CustomFormatResource> CustomFormats`, always set via `model.RemoteEpisode?.CustomFormats?.ToResource(false)` at `QueueResource.cs:75` + `CustomFormatScore = model.Series?.QualityProfile?.Value?.CalculateCustomFormatScore(customFormats) ?? 0`. J349 adds the missing field (`CustomFormats []CustomFormatResource json:"customFormats,omitempty"` — omitempty drops the key when no formats, matching the C# null-list-absent posture) and projects both CustomFormats + CustomFormatScore in `projectQueueResource` from the queue row's `RemoteEpisode *decisionengine.Subject` (the Subject carries the decision-time-matched CustomFormats slice + the precomputed CustomFormatScore). The Go-port reuses the Subject's precomputed score rather than recomputing against the current series QP as C# does — the two are equivalent unless the QP changed between grab and display, and reusing it avoids threading customFormatsSvc + qualityProfilesSvc lookups into the queue paged adapter (documented in the QueueResource doc-comment). CustomFormats is NOT gated by an include flag (C# always sets it). 2 new adapter tests (CustomFormats+Score projection from a populated Subject; no-RemoteEpisode → CustomFormats nil + Score 0). gofmt re-aligned the struct + adapter after the field insertion. Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/queue/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (v3 2.688s, v5/queue 2.137s, cmd/sonarr 3.831s); `go vet` + gofmt clean. Post-J342 service-port arc: Arc 1 (J343) + Arc 2 (J344-J346) DONE; Arc 3 IN PROGRESS — scalar paged surface (J347) + Series/Episode hydration (J348) + CustomFormats/Score (J349) landed; remaining Arc 3 sub-slices are StatusMessages projection (lossy []string→[]QueueStatusMessage synthesis design question) and the v5-only Episodes-plural + EpisodeIDs + SeasonNumbers hydration + includeSubresources[] enum-array query-param gating.

HEAD at `J348` — **Post-J342 service-port arc, slice 6 (Arc 3 slice 2, B-category): Queue Series + Episode (singular) subresource hydration with v3 includeSeries/includeEpisode gating**. Hydrates the two J322-pinned QueueResource subresources off the domain objects the queue.Queue row already carries (no new lookups). C# `QueueResourceMapper.ToResource(model, includeSeries, includeEpisode)` at `src/Sonarr.Api.V3/Queue/QueueResource.cs:73-74` gates `Series = includeSeries && model.Series != null ? model.Series.ToResource() : null` and `Episode = includeEpisode && model.Episode != null ? ...`; both flags default false per `QueueController.cs:141`. The Go-port uses a hydrate-then-strip posture: `projectQueueResource` (cmd/sonarr) hydrates `Series` via `apiv3.ToSeriesResource(*q.Series)` + `Episode` (singular) via `apiv3.ToEpisodeResource(q.Episodes[0])` unconditionally (keeping the QueuePagedLister interface free of wire-shape concerns), and `apiv3.QueueHandler` parses `includeSeries` + `includeEpisode` query params (default false) and strips each record's subresource back to nil when the corresponding flag is absent. v5/queue.ListHandler remains aliased to the v3 handler — v5 clients send the `includeSubresources[]` enum-array (not includeSeries/includeEpisode), so v5 currently always strips → no subresources → no regression; v5's enum-array gating is the J349 slice. The J347 adapter test's subresources-must-be-nil guard was updated to assert Series + Episode are now hydrated (Episodes plural stays nil at the adapter level). 1 new v3 handler test (`TestQueueHandler_SubresourceGating_J348`) with 3 sub-tests: no flags strips both; includeSeries=true keeps series + strips episode; both flags keep both. Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/queue/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (v3 2.105s, v5/queue 1.527s, cmd/sonarr 4.672s); `go vet` clean. Post-J342 service-port arc: Arc 1 (J343) + Arc 2 (J344-J346) DONE; Arc 3 IN PROGRESS — scalar paged surface (J347) + Series/Episode hydration (J348) landed; remaining Arc 3 sub-slices are CustomFormats/Score + StatusMessages projection, the v5-only Episodes-plural + EpisodeIDs + SeasonNumbers hydration, and the v5 includeSubresources[] enum-array query-param gating.

HEAD at `J347` — **Post-J342 service-port arc, slice 5 (Arc 3 slice 1, B-category): Queue.Service paged surface — scalar QueueResource projection + paging adapter wired to /api/v3/queue + /api/v5/queue**. Opens Arc 3 (the largest of the three arcs). Replaces the `emptyQueuePagedLister{}` stub on both queue list routes with a real `queuePagedAdapter` that reads the in-memory `queue.Service.GetQueue()` cache, paginates in cache order (matching the historyPagedAdapter convention — sorting stays DEFERRED), and projects each `queue.Queue` domain row to a scalar `apiv3.QueueResource`. C# source at `src/Sonarr.Api.V3/Queue/QueueResource.cs:58-107` (`QueueResourceMapper.ToResource(model, includeSeries, includeEpisode)`) drives the field mapping. The new `projectQueueResource(q queue.Queue) apiv3.QueueResource` helper maps the scalar field set: ID, SeriesID (from q.Series.ID), EpisodeID (from q.Episodes[0].ID — the Go domain carries Episodes[] where C# has singular model.Episode), Title, Status (CamelCaseEnumName for the JsonStringEnumConverter camelCase policy), DownloadID, Protocol (lowercase String() form), Languages (extract persisted IDs → ToLanguageRefs), Quality (ToQualityModelResource), TrackedDownloadStatus/State (CamelCaseEnumName when the pointer is non-nil), Size, SizeLeft, TimeLeft (FormatTimeLeft), Indexer, DownloadClient, DownloadClientHasPostImportCategory, OutputPath, ErrorMessage, Added, EstimatedCompletionTime. The Series / Episode / Episodes / EpisodeIDs / SeasonNumbers / StatusMessages subresources + CustomFormats + CustomFormatScore are left nil/zero — DEFERRED to the J348+ hydration slices (the queue.Queue row already carries the Series + Episodes domain objects, so hydration is projection-only — no new lookups needed). The now-dead `emptyQueuePagedLister` type was removed (CLAUDE.md dead-code policy) and the emptyQueueDetailsLister doc-comment's dangling reference fixed. 3 new tests in cmd/sonarr/queue_paged_adapter_test.go (empty queue → empty page; scalar projection correctness pinning Status="downloading"/Protocol="torrent"/TimeLeft="01:30:00"/SeriesID/EpisodeID/Languages + subresources-nil guard; pagination page-2-of-25). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./cmd/sonarr/... ./internal/api/v5/queue/... ./internal/api/v3/...` all pass (cmd/sonarr 3.841s, v5/queue 2.649s, v3 2.264s); `go vet` clean. Post-J342 service-port arc backlog: Arc 1 (J343) + Arc 2 (J344-J346) DONE; Arc 3 IN PROGRESS — scalar paged surface landed at J347; remaining Arc 3 sub-slices are Series subresource hydration, Episode/Episodes subresource hydration, CustomFormats/Score, StatusMessages projection, and the v5 includeSubresources[] enum-array query-param gating.

HEAD at `J346` — **Post-J342 service-port arc, slice 4 (Arc 2 slice 3 — DRAINS Arc 2, A-category): ManualImport reprocess handler populated CustomFormats + CustomFormatScore via the J345 calculator reused behind a new ManualImportCustomFormatCalculator optional-extension**. Closes the last Arc 2 sub-slice — the `ManualImport handler populated customFormats` row deferred at J238. The reprocess handler already parses the item path into a ParsedEpisodeInfo, resolves languages, and carries IndexerFlags + SeriesID — exactly the inputs the J345 `customformats.ParseCustomFormatForRemoteEpisode` calculator needs, so J346 reuses it rather than waiting on the full ImportDecisionMaker port. C# source at `src/Sonarr.Api.V3/ManualImport/ManualImportController.cs:62-63` sets `item.CustomFormats = processedItem.CustomFormats.ToResource(false)` + `item.CustomFormatScore = processedItem.CustomFormatScore` from `_manualImportService.ReprocessItem(...)` which runs the LocalEpisode calculator; the Go-port approximates the release-title-derivable subset (resolution / codec / release-group specs) — the file-size + mediainfo specs stay deferred because the bare-path endpoint can't synthesise those LocalEpisode fields without ImportDecisionMaker. New `apiv3.ManualImportCustomFormatCalculator interface { CalculateForManualImport(ctx, *parser.ParsedEpisodeInfo, seriesID int, languages []language.Language, indexerFlags int) ([]CustomFormatResource, int) }`. `ManualImportReprocessHandler` gains a 3rd positional optional dep `calc ManualImportCustomFormatCalculator` threaded into `reprocessOne`; after computing item.Languages, when `calc != nil && item.SeriesID > 0`, converts item.Languages (LanguageRef→language.Language via new `languagesFromLanguageRefs` helper + language.FindByID) and invokes the calculator — assigns item.CustomFormats + item.CustomFormatScore. Nil calc / no series → pre-J346 empty-slice fallback preserved. cmd/sonarr `parsingServiceWithPopulator` (J345 wrapper) gains a `CalculateForManualImport` method that resolves the series via seriesSvc, fetches allFormats, runs ParseCustomFormatForRemoteEpisode (size=0, IndexerFlags from the int param), projects + computes the score via Series.QualityProfileID. Both manualimport POST routes (v3 + v5 alias) now pass `parsingSvcWithPopulator` as the 3rd arg. v5/manualimport.ReprocessHandler is a direct `var` alias of the v3 handler so the signature change propagates automatically. 3 new v3 tests (nil-calc empty CustomFormats backwards-compat + calc-populates with typed CF + score:25 + calc-not-called-without-seriesId) + 1 new fake (fakeManualImportCalc). 18 existing v3 reprocess test call sites + 2 v5 sites updated to pass nil for the new arg via replace_all. Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/manualimport/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (v3 1.729s, v5/manualimport 2.110s, cmd/sonarr 4.022s); `go vet` clean. **Arc 2 (parsingService.Map + ImportDecisionMaker) DRAINS at J346** — Parse handler fully populated (Series/Episodes/Languages J344 + CustomFormats J345) + ManualImport CustomFormats J346. The full ImportDecisionMaker spec pipeline (Rejections list + file-size/mediainfo CF specs) remains a separate future arc, not part of the populated-mapper backlog. Post-J342 service-port arc: Arc 1 (J343) + Arc 2 (J344-J346) DONE; Arc 3 (Queue.Service paged surface + QueueResource v5 includeSubresources[] hydration) is the only remaining arc.

HEAD at `J345` — **Post-J342 service-port arc, slice 3 (Arc 2 slice 2, A-category): Parse handler populated CustomFormats + CustomFormatScore via new ParseCustomFormatForRemoteEpisode helper + ParsePopulator optional-extension type-assertion (J338/J339/J342 idiom)**. Layers the CustomFormats projection onto the J344 Map wire-through, completing the v3 + v5 Parse handler's populated-row mapper. ManualImport handler is the only Arc 2 sub-slice remaining; it's the larger port (~3 service deps + decision-maker integration). C# source at `src/Sonarr.Api.V3/Parse/ParseController.cs:53-54` calls `_formatCalculator.ParseCustomFormat(remoteEpisode, 0)` + `series.QualityProfile.Value.CalculateCustomFormatScore(remoteEpisode.CustomFormats) ?? 0` after the Map step. The Go-port equivalent: a NEW `customformats.ParseCustomFormatForRemoteEpisode(parsedInfo, ser, languages, indexerFlags, size, allFormats) []CustomFormat` helper in `internal/customformats/calculate_remoteepisode.go` that mirrors C# `CustomFormatCalculationService.ParseCustomFormat(RemoteEpisode, long)` at `:36-49`. The helper takes individual-field parameters instead of a *decisionengine.Subject pointer because the customformats package already imports nothing from decisionengine — a Subject-typed parameter would create an import cycle (decisionengine itself imports customformats for Subject.CustomFormats). Callers in cmd/sonarr + the API layer hold the Subject and pass the lifted fields. Empty allFormats / nil parsedInfo returns nil. New `apiv3.ParsePopulator interface { PopulateParseCustomFormats(ctx, *decisionengine.Subject) ([]CustomFormatResource, int) }` declares the optional-extension that wraps the calculator engine + score lookup. Both `apiv3.ParseHandler` + `parsev5.GetHandler` type-assert the mapper for `ParsePopulator` after a successful Map projection; populator non-nil → populates `CustomFormats` + `CustomFormatScore`; populator nil → CustomFormats stays nil (omitempty drops it) + Score stays 0 (matches the J344 minimal-shape deferred state verbatim). cmd/sonarr/host_wiring.go gains a NEW `parsingServiceWithPopulator` wrapper struct that embeds *parsingservice.Service (preserves the full ParseMapper surface via embedding) + holds historySeriesLookup + historyQualityProfileLookup + historyCustomFormatsLookup deps (reuses the J336 lookup interfaces for symmetry with the history + episodefile populator wrappers). Its `PopulateParseCustomFormats` method runs the C# `:36-49` sequence: fetch allFormats via customformats.Service.All; build inputs from subject.ParsedEpisodeInfo + Series + Languages + IndexerFlags-from-Release; call the new helper; project to []CustomFormatResource + compute score via qualityprofiles.CalculateCustomFormatScore against the Series.QualityProfileID. The wrapper is constructed once in v3Routes alongside historySvcPopulated + episodeFilesSvcPopulated. Both Parse routes now pass `parsingSvcWithPopulator` instead of bare `parsingSvc`. New `parser` import in host_wiring.go for the IndexerFlags lookup. 3 new unit tests for the helper in `calculate_remoteepisode_test.go` (nil parsedInfo + empty formats + matches-release-title-spec + size-threads-through). 3 new sub-tests in v3 parse_test.go covering the populator extension (mapper-without-populator falls through to empty CustomFormats; mapper-with-populator emits populated customFormats[] + customFormatScore:42; nil-Series subject short-circuits BEFORE the populator branch). The existing 5 J344 tests stay green — their fakeParseMapper doesn't implement ParsePopulator so the type-assertion returns nil and the empty fallback fires. Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/customformats/... ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/parse/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (customformats 19.877s, v3 1.583s, v5/parse 1.992s, cmd/sonarr 4.599s); `go vet` clean. Arc 3 (Queue.Service paged surface) still OPEN.

HEAD at `J344` — **Post-J342 service-port arc, slice 2 (Arc 2 slice 1, A-category): Parse handler populated Series + Episodes + Languages via parsingService.Map wire-through (the smaller half of the parsingService.Map + ImportDecisionMaker arc; CustomFormats projection deferred to J345)**. Opens Arc 2 of the service-port arc — the Go-port `parsingservice.Service.Map` was already implemented (`internal/parsingservice/parsingservice.go:1187`) but the Parse + ManualImport handlers never wired it through; this slice does the Parse-handler wire-through for the Series/Episodes/Languages fields. The CustomFormats half remains deferred because it needs the `aggregation.Service.Augment` pre-pass + a new `customformats.ParseCustomFormatForRemoteEpisode` helper that builds a `CustomFormatInput` from a `decisionengine.Subject` (mirrors C# `CustomFormatCalculationService.ParseCustomFormat(RemoteEpisode, long)` at `:36-49`). ManualImport handler is the larger sub-slice still untouched in this slice. C# source at `src/Sonarr.Api.V3/Parse/ParseController.cs:31-77` calls `_parsingService.Map(parsedEpisodeInfo, 0, 0, null) → RemoteEpisode → projects Title + ParsedEpisodeInfo + Series.ToResource + Episodes.ToResource + Languages`; this slice gets the Go-port Parse handler to the same wire shape minus CustomFormats. `ParseResource` widened with `Series *SeriesResource json:"series,omitempty"` + `Episodes []EpisodeResource json:"episodes,omitempty"` + `Languages []language.Language json:"languages,omitempty"` — all `omitempty` to preserve byte-equivalence with pre-J344 minimal-shape callers (C# emits these via `JsonIgnore(WhenWritingNull)` on nullable `List<>` fields; omitempty drops the key when nil). New narrow `apiv3.ParseMapper interface { Map(ctx, *parser.ParsedEpisodeInfo, int, int, string, *decisionengine.SearchCriteria) (*decisionengine.Subject, error) }` satisfied by `*parsingservice.Service` via Go structural typing — no adapter at the wire site. Both `apiv3.ParseHandler` and `parsev5.GetHandler` gain a single new positional optional dep `mapper ParseMapper` after the existing parameter list (both were zero-arg before). When mapper is non-nil AND parsing yielded a non-nil ParsedEpisodeInfo, calls `mapper.Map(ctx, parsed, 0, 0, "", nil)`; on non-nil Subject with non-nil Series, projects via `ToSeriesResource` + `ToEpisodeResource` + `subject.Languages`. Nil mapper / nil Subject / nil Series / Map error all degrade silently to the pre-J344 minimal shape — backwards-compatible for every existing test fixture. cmd/sonarr/host_wiring.go threads `parsingSvc` to both Parse routes (`/api/v3/parse` + `/api/v5/parse`); parsingSvc was already in scope from J343. 5 new v3 tests (nil-mapper backwards compat + happy-path populated + nil-Subject fallback + nil-Series fallback + Map-error silent degradation) + 1 new v5 test (happy-path populated, exercises the v5 GetHandler signature change). Existing v3/v5 test call sites updated to pass `nil` for the new arg (4 v3 sites + 4 v5 sites via replace_all). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/parse/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (v3 1.764s, v5/parse 2.652s, cmd/sonarr 4.331s); `go vet ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/parse/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` clean. Post-J342 service-port arc backlog: 1 of 3 arcs DONE (J343, Release POST searchInfo fallback); Arc 2 (parsingService.Map + ImportDecisionMaker) IN PROGRESS — Parse-handler Series/Episodes/Languages half landed at J344, CustomFormats half + ManualImport handler remain. Arc 3 (Queue.Service paged surface) still OPEN.

HEAD at `J343` — **Post-J342 service-port arc, slice 1 (B-category): Release POST searchInfo fallback ladder ported via narrow-interface wire-through of *parsingservice.Service.GetEpisodes + *episodes.Service.GetEpisode**. Opens the new service-port arc by closing the `Release POST searchInfo fallback` row from the J342 backlog. C# `src/Sonarr.Api.V3/Indexers/ReleaseController.cs:114-153` defines the fallback ladder that fires when the cached `RemoteEpisode.Series` is nil OR `RemoteEpisode.Episodes` is empty: tries `release.EpisodeId`+single-episode lookup, falls back to `release.SeriesId`+`_parsingService.GetEpisodes(parsedInfo, series, sceneSource=true)`, else 404s with one of two distinct messages ("Unable to find matching series and episodes" when neither id supplied; "Unable to parse episodes in the release" when parsing/single-episode also fail). The v5 equivalent at `src/Sonarr.Api.V5/Release/ReleaseController.cs:135-159` is driven by the `searchInfo` body sub-block (`seriesId` + `episodeId` fields). Both Go-port surfaces were previously dispatching the cached Subject verbatim — when the cached state happened to be incomplete the dispatch would silently bypass the recovery path. Two new narrow interfaces declared in `internal/api/v3/release_write.go`: `ReleaseParsingFallback { GetEpisodes(ctx, *parser.ParsedEpisodeInfo, *series.Series, bool) ([]episodes.Episode, error) }` satisfied by *parsingservice.Service via Go structural typing; `ReleaseSingleEpisodeLookup { GetEpisode(ctx, int) (*episodes.Episode, error) }` satisfied by *episodes.Service. ReleaseDownloadHandler signature extended with these 2 new positional deps after the existing 4 — both nil-tolerant (well-formed cached Subject fast-paths regardless; broken Subject + nil deps surfaces as 503 ServiceUnavailable matching the override-branch pattern at J249). New `applyParsingFallback(ctx, body, decision, seriesLookup, singleEpLookup, parsingFallback) (Decision, int, string)` helper ports the 4 fallback arms verbatim from C#: Subject.Series==nil + body.EpisodeId>0 → single-episode lookup + series-by-episode-SeriesID lookup; Subject.Series==nil + body.SeriesId>0 → series lookup + parsing fallback with sceneSource=true (404 on empty result with the C# `:130` message); Subject.Series==nil + neither id → 404 with the C# `:138` message; Subject.Series!=nil + Episodes empty → parsing fallback + optional single-episode fallback when parsing returns empty and body.EpisodeId>0; final guard → 404 with the C# `:155` message. The cloned Subject preserves Release / SceneMapping / MappedSeasonNumber / CustomFormats / etc. via shallow struct copy (matches the J249 override-clone pattern at C# `:93-104`) so the cache stays pristine — cache invariant pin in `TestReleaseDownloadHandler_Fallback_CachedSubjectNotMutated`. v5/release/release.go `DownloadHandler` signature extended with the same 2 deps + threads them to the v3 handler; v5 body translation gains `body.SearchInfo.SeriesID/EpisodeID → v3Body.SeriesID/EpisodeID` so the v3 ladder fires against the v5 wire shape (DEFERRED comments on the v5 file dropped). cmd/sonarr `buildHostServer` + `v3Routes` signatures grew a `parsingSvc *parsingservice.Service` parameter; production wire sites pass `parsingSvc, episodesSvc` (episodesSvc satisfies both `ReleaseParsingFallback` via *parsingservice.Service and `ReleaseSingleEpisodeLookup` via the Service.GetEpisode method directly). 8 new tests covering all 4 fallback arms + the 503 nil-deps case + the cache-pristine invariant in v3; 1 new test (`TestDownloadHandler_SearchInfo_SeriesIDRoutesToV3Fallback`) in v5/release pinning the searchInfo wire-through. 2 existing tests (CacheHitDispatches in v3 + CacheHit_EchoesV5BodyAndDispatches in v5) updated to fixture well-formed Subjects (Series + Episodes populated) — previously those tests would have failed the C# fallback even pre-J343 if exercised at the controller level; the pre-J343 Go-port silently bypassed the recovery. Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/release/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (v3 1.7s, v5/release 2.4s, cmd/sonarr 4.0s); `go vet ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/release/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` clean. HEAD at `J309` — **Post-Wave-3 drain, slice 1: v5 SeasonResource Statistics + Images pointer-field widening (J302 pattern)**. The Wave 3 arc DRAINED at J308; this slice opens the post-Wave-3 backlog by closing the smallest queued struct-pointer widening — the J307 follow-up explicitly cited: "Statistics + Images intentionally omitted from the struct today — adding them later as `*Statistics` + `*[]MediaCover` pointer-to-slice (J302 pattern) preserves the WhenWritingNull wire posture without breaking this single-monitor endpoint." `internal/api/v5/series/series.go` extends `SeasonResource` from 2 declared fields → 4: adds `Statistics *SeasonStatistics` + `Images *[]MediaCover` both with `json:",omitempty"`. New `SeasonStatistics` struct (11 fields) mirrors `src/Sonarr.Api.V5/Series/SeasonStatisticsResource.cs:7-21` — v5 adds `MonitoredEpisodeCount`, `ReleaseTypes`, `EpisodeFileQualities` over the v3 shape. Non-nullable scalars (EpisodeFileCount, EpisodeCount, TotalEpisodeCount, MonitoredEpisodeCount, SizeOnDisk, PercentOfEpisodes) always emit when the parent Statistics pointer is non-nil; nullable slices (`ReleaseGroups`, `ReleaseTypes`, `EpisodeFileQualities`) use omitempty so nil drops the key (matches `JsonIgnoreCondition.WhenWritingNull`). `ReleaseTypes` is `[]string` carrying the camelCase enum strings ("singleEpisode"/"multiEpisode"/"seasonPack"/"unknown") that STJson's global `JsonStringEnumConverter(CamelCase)` produces. `EpisodeFileQualities` is `[]json.RawMessage` — the Quality DTO is complex enough that a typed projection belongs to the populated-mapper port arc, not the wire-pin slice. `PercentOfEpisodes` is a stored float64 — C# computes it (returns 0 when EpisodeCount=0); the Go-port leaves the computation to whichever populated-mapper allocates the struct. New `MediaCover` struct (CoverType string + Url string + RemoteUrl string with omitempty) is v5-local — the existing `mediacover.Cover` keeps `CoverType` as a typed int (the Series.Images JSON blob persists the legacy int representation), while v5's STJson wires the enum as camelCase string. The two types stay decoupled: the populated-mapper translates `mediacover.Cover.CoverType.String()` → this struct's `CoverType` string field at projection time. **No production caller populates these fields today** — the SeasonMonitorHandler still emits `SeasonResource{SeasonNumber, Monitored}` so both pointers stay nil and omitempty drops the keys; the existing positive-pin test (`TestSeasonMonitorHandler_Success_Returns200WithBodyAndUpdatesSeasonsJSON`) continues to pass with its `must NOT contain "images"`/`must NOT contain "statistics"` negative assertions. 4 new wire-shape tests in `series_test.go`: `TestSeasonResource_NilStatisticsAndImages_OmitKeys` (canonical nil-pointer → key-absent pin); `TestSeasonResource_PopulatedStatistics_EmitsFullNestedShape` (pinned `2026-01-02T03:04:05Z` date + 11-field nested shape with mixed nil-and-populated child fields — covers PreviousAiring + EpisodeFileQualities nil-omitempty within the populated parent); `TestSeasonResource_EmptyImagesSlice_EmitsEmptyArray` (`&[]MediaCover{}` → `"images":[]` — the 3-state pointer-to-slice contract that distinguishes "explicitly empty" from "absent"); `TestSeasonResource_PopulatedImages_EmitsCoverEntriesWithStringCoverType` (CoverType string-enum pin + RemoteUrl-omitempty pin via `strings.Count == 1` defensive assertion). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go vet ./internal/api/v5/series/... ./cmd/sonarr/...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v5/series/... ./internal/api/v3/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (v5/series 1.637s — 16 tests now: 3 CRUD-DELETE + 4 Editor + 5 SeasonMonitor + 4 wire-shape pin; v3 1.581s; cmd/sonarr 3.692s). Wire-shape pin only; populated allocation arrives with the post-Wave-3 SeriesStatistics service-port arc.

The remaining piece per the autonomous-loop scope was "Resource projection alignment with the calculator-engine output that's now wired (J295-J300)". This slice closes that: `apiv3.CustomFormatResource.Specifications` widened from `[]CustomFormatSpecificationSchema` to `*[]CustomFormatSpecificationSchema` with `json:"specifications,omitempty"` — the pointer-to-slice idiom that distinguishes nil (lite/calculator projection — `omitempty` drops the key) from non-nil-empty (full projection — Go follows the pointer + marshals `[]`). New exported constructor `apiv3.ToCustomFormatResourceLite(f *customformats.Format)` emits the `includeDetails=false` shape (`{"id":N, "name":"X"}` — both `IncludeCustomFormatWhenRenaming` AND `Specifications` stay nil so the omitempty tags drop both keys), matching C# `new CustomFormatResource { Id = model.Id, Name = model.Name }` at `CustomFormatResource.cs:21-25` under Sonarr's STJson global `DefaultIgnoreCondition.WhenWritingNull` config (`src/NzbDrone.Common/Serializer/System.Text.Json/STJson.cs:27`). Used by the calculator engine chain — `formatCalculator.ParseCustomFormat(model, model.Series).ToResource(false)` at `EpisodeFileResource.cs:46-66` (J298 widening anchor) — when populated-row service mappers land post-Wave-3, their `customFormats[]` entries on EpisodeFile/Episode/Calendar/Wanted/History/Blocklist/ManualImport/Parse/Log carrier resources will allocate via this constructor without further struct surgery. Existing `toCustomFormatResource` constructor (full projection) preserved verbatim — still allocates `&[]CustomFormatSpecificationSchema{}` so the GET list / by-id / POST / PUT / PUT-bulk wire shapes remain `"specifications":[]` exactly (existing `TestCustomFormatHandler_OK` at customformat_test.go:44 still pins this). 3 call-site updates in `validateCustomFormat` (`len(res.Specifications) == 0` → `res.Specifications == nil || len(*res.Specifications) == 0`; range loop now over `*res.Specifications`) + `customFormatToModel` (Marshal target switched to `*res.Specifications`). 4 new tests: `TestToCustomFormatResourceLite_EmitsIDAndNameOnly` (positive id+name pins + negative pins on both optional keys — wire byte-shape match for the calculator projection); `TestToCustomFormatResourceLite_NilFormat_EmitsZeroValues` (defensive — nil Format yields `id:0`/name:"" with both optional fields still omitted); `TestToCustomFormatResource_FullProjection_StillEmitsEmptySpecsArray` (regression guard pinning that the J302 pointer-switch did NOT break the existing full-projection wire shape — `"specifications":[]` AND `"includeCustomFormatWhenRenaming"` both still emitted); `TestCustomFormatResource_RoundTrip_NilSpecsDecodesAsNilPointer` (validates that 3 input shapes — missing key, `null` value, `[]` value — all collapse to nil-or-empty under the pointer-to-slice tag, so the validator's "Must contain at least one Condition" error fires for all three uniformly per the C# `.NotEmpty()` rule). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go vet ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/customformat/...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/customformat/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (v3 1.7s, v5/customformat 2.3s, cmd/sonarr 4.3s). HEAD at `J301` — Wave 3 LARGE-family v5 endpoint arc, **slice 1 of 5: EpisodeFile family** (smallest LOC; 205 LOC C# — `src/Sonarr.Api.V5/EpisodeFiles/EpisodeFileController.cs`). Six v5 routes wired in `host_wiring.go` v5Routes() under the new `episodefilev5` import alias, immediately after the episode v5 block. HEAD at `J295` — Wave 1 custom-format calculator arc, **final slice: J273 LogResource `method` field omission on v5**. The calculator-engine sub-slices 1-4 of the loop scope (spec evaluator port, ParsedEpisodeInfo→CustomFormatInput projection, Calculate() service, qualityprofiles.CalculateCustomFormatScore adapter) all landed pre-J251 — sub-slices 5 (handler wire-through) and 6 (LogResource method omission) were the two outstanding items. This slice drains item 6. v5's `LogResource` per `src/Sonarr.Api.V5/Logs/LogResource.cs` carries 7 fields (id/time/exception?/exceptionType?/level/logger/message) where v3's carries 8 (adds `method?`). The J273 path-alias deferred the field-divergence — invisible at empty-corpus because v3 LogHandler returns `records:[]`. This slice replaces the `type Resource = apiv3.LogResource` alias with a v5-specific 7-field struct (no Method) AND wraps the v3 LogHandler with a `stripMethod` middleware that JSON-decodes the paged envelope, removes the `method` key from each record under `records[]`, and re-emits. Strip is a no-op at empty-corpus (records `[]`); when the log DB plumbing lands and v3 starts emitting populated records with `method`, the v5 wire stays 7-field clean without further changes. Non-200 status / non-object body / no-records-key bodies all pass through verbatim — failure-path envelopes (400/500) preserved. New `stripMethod` helper kept inline for the first consumer (J283 first-consumer rule — same posture as the GeneralSettings stripField helper); consolidates into v5util only if a second paged-record-field-strip need appears. v5 LogHandler changes from a `var = apiv3.LogHandler` alias to a `func LogHandler() http.Handler { return stripMethod(apiv3.LogHandler()) }` wrapper. host_wiring.go already calls `logv5.LogHandler()` so no route changes needed. Test file gains 7 new tests: Resource-omits-method-field (reflect-based pin), Resource-json-omits-method-key, stripMethod-removes-method-from-each-record (canonical populated-records pin via 2-row fake upstream), stripMethod-empty-records-noop, stripMethod-non-200-passes-through (500 + method-bearing error envelope preserved), stripMethod-non-object-body-passes-through (`null` body), stripMethod-no-records-key-passes-through (envelope-level `method` preserved when records[] absent). Removed: 2 type-alias tests (Resource/v3.LogResource roundtrip — no longer valid since Resource is a distinct struct). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go vet ./internal/api/v5/log/... ./cmd/sonarr/...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v5/log/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (1.7s + 3.7s). Wave 1 custom-format calculator arc DRAINED — sub-slices 1-4 pre-existed, sub-slice 5 wire-through is broader cross-cutting work tracked separately (per-handler scope across Parse/Update/Blocklist/Wanted/Calendar/History/ManualImport), sub-slice 6 lands here.

HEAD at `J291` — Wave 3 fortieth slice: v5 **QueueStatus** family port (80 LOC C# — QueueStatusController only; single GET /api/v5/queue/status endpoint that drives the React UI's top-bar queue badge with counts + warning/error booleans). Pure path-alias slice (J224/J280/J290 template). v3 already exposes /api/v3/queue/status via apiv3.QueueStatusHandler with matching field-by-field wire shape (7 fields: totalCount, count, unknownCount, errors, warnings, unknownErrors, unknownWarnings), status (200), and SignalR integration via the shared Pauser debouncer-bracket pattern. SMALL WIRE DIVERGENCE noted (potential, not confirmed): v5 openapi schema includes `id` as a property (inherited RestResource.Id); if the C# RestResource has [JsonIgnore(WhenWritingDefault)] on Id the wire is identical to v3, otherwise v5 emits `"id":0` while v3 Go-port's QueueStatusResource struct doesn't declare an Id field. Logged as a v3-side audit follow-up; current slice ships the path-alias for the common-case path. SignalR: v5 controller declares IHandle<QueueUpdatedEvent> + IHandle<PendingReleasesUpdatedEvent> with a 5-second Debouncer driving Updated broadcasts; v3 Go-port wires equivalent broadcasts via the bus subscription in cmd/sonarr/signalr_wiring.go — carries through unchanged. The Pauser debouncer-bracket pattern (Pause around the GET projection, Resume after) prevents the GET handler's own projection from re-arming the broadcaster debouncer. New `internal/api/v5/queuestatus/` package (~60 LOC source + ~135 LOC test): 4 type aliases (Resource = apiv3.QueueStatusResource, QueueLister = apiv3.QueueLister, PendingLister = apiv3.PendingLister, Pauser = apiv3.Pauser) + 1 variable alias (GetHandler = apiv3.QueueStatusHandler). NO new handler code. 6 tests: GetHandler-empty-queue-all-zeroes, GetHandler-with-pending-count (5 pending → TotalCount=5+Count=5), GetHandler-pending-error-treated-as-zero (defensive read posture — pending err does NOT 500), GetHandler-pauser-bracketed (Pause+Resume both called exactly once), GetHandler-nil-pauser-tolerated (nil-safe), Resource-type-alias compile-time pin. 1 new route wired alongside the existing /api/v3/queue/status route in cmd/sonarr/host_wiring.go (re-uses queueSvc + pendingCountLister + queueStatusPauser deps verbatim). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go vet`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v5/queuestatus/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (1.3s + 3.7s). v5 surface coverage 43/~50 → 44/~50.

HEAD at `J283` — Wave 3 thirty-second slice: v5 **GeneralSettings** family port (207 LOC C# across GeneralSettingsController + GeneralSettingsResource; uses shared SettingsController<T> base). 3 endpoints: GET /api/v5/settings/general, GET /api/v5/settings/general/{id}, PUT /api/v5/settings/general/{id}. ONE wire-shape divergence from v3 HostConfigController: v5 OMITS the `trustCgnatIpAddresses` field (newer field added to v3 only — post-v5-fork). Other 37 fields are present on both v3 and v5 with byte-identical JSON tag spelling and types. Status codes match v3 directly: GET → 200, PUT → 202 Accepted. Strategy: wrap v3's HostConfigHandler + HostConfigUpdateHandler with a field-strip middleware (new private `stripField` helper in the v5 generalsettings package) that buffers the v3 response, JSON-decodes the body, deletes the trustCgnatIpAddresses key, and re-marshals before forwarding. v3's DisallowUnknownFields decoder accepts v5-shaped POST bodies because the only missing field defaults to `false` (zero value) — indistinguishable from an explicit `trustCgnatIpAddresses:false` send. Error bodies (400/404/500), non-JSON responses, and non-success status codes all pass through unchanged. The C# v5 controller adds a user-upsert SaveSettings override (when Username+Password both set, _userService.Upsert called before base SaveSettings) which v3 Go-port doesn't carry — DEFERRED on the v5 Go-port surface as well; pending an IUserService Go port (tracked as v5 follow-up gap). Validation chain in C# v5 is substantially richer than v3 Go-port (FileExistsValidator + IsValidCertificate chain on SslCertPath when EnableSsl, FileExistsValidator on SslKeyPath, PasswordConfirmation match-against-stored, AuthenticationMethod NotEqual(Basic) with custom error message) — same DEFERRED state as the J282 MediaManagement deep validators, pending the disk-validator + IUserService adapter shims. Tests use internal-test package style (same `generalsettings` namespace) to access the unexported stripField helper directly. 3 new routes wired alongside the existing /api/v3/config/host routes in cmd/sonarr/host_wiring.go (re-uses `hostCfg` adapter declared at line 1033 — same composite-provider that backs v3). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go vet`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v5/generalsettings/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (1.3s + 3.7s). v5 surface coverage 35/~50 → 36/~50.

NO new handler code. 8 tests: GetHandler-emits-singleton (id=1 + theme=auto + UILanguage=1 default), GetHandler-theme-error-500 (verifies Theme() error path surfaces 500 not 200), UpdateHandler-put-accepted-persists-all-10-fields (round-trip + 10 setter calls counted via mutex-protected fake), UpdateHandler-uiLanguage-zero-400 (< 1 rejected), UpdateHandler-uiLanguage-unknown-400 (99999 not in Language.All), UpdateHandler-rejects-unknown-field-400 (DisallowUnknownFields), variable-aliases-non-nil, type-aliases compile-time pin. fakeUI uses mutex-protected counters for -race cleanliness and satisfies the full apiv3.UIConfigWriter interface (10 getters + 10 setters + Theme err path). 3 new routes wired alongside the existing /api/v3/config/ui routes in cmd/sonarr/host_wiring.go (re-uses `uiCfg` adapter declared at line 1030 — same dep v3 uses). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go vet`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v5/uisettings/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (1.3s + 3.7s). v5 surface coverage 33/~50 → 34/~50.

HEAD at `J280` — Wave 3 twenty-ninth slice: v5 **IndexerSettings** family port (57 LOC C# across IndexerSettingsController + IndexerSettingsResource; uses shared SettingsController<T> base). 3 endpoints: GET /api/v5/settings/indexer, GET /api/v5/settings/indexer/{id}, PUT /api/v5/settings/indexer/{id}. Pure path-alias slice — same J224 importlistsettings pattern, much simpler than J279 UpdateSettings because v3 already has an `IndexerConfigController` with a byte-identical wire shape (mounted at /api/v3/config/indexer). Resource: 5 fields (id/minimumAge/retention/maximumSize/rssSyncInterval), all int, all on IConfigService. Status: GET → 200, PUT → 202 Accepted — both match v3's IndexerConfigHandler / IndexerConfigUpdateHandler directly. Validation chain (MinimumAge ≥ 0, Retention ≥ 0, MaximumSize ≥ 0, RssSyncInterval ∈ {0} ∪ [10, 120]) lives in v3's validateIndexerConfig — preserved verbatim via the path-alias. RssSyncInterval=0 is the "disable RSS" sentinel and bypasses the [10, 120] range check (test pins this). Negative values 400. DisallowUnknownFields rejects unknown body keys. The C# v5 SettingsController uses reflection-based SaveConfigDictionary; the Go-port writes through the typed SetMinimumAge / SetRetention / SetMaximumSize / SetRssSyncInterval setters which produce byte-identical results. New `internal/api/v5/indexersettings/` package (~50 LOC source + ~150 LOC test): 3 type aliases (Resource = apiv3.IndexerConfigResource, Getter = apiv3.IndexerConfigGetter, Writer = apiv3.IndexerConfigWriter) + 2 variable aliases (GetHandler = apiv3.IndexerConfigHandler, UpdateHandler = apiv3.IndexerConfigUpdateHandler). NO new handler code. 7 tests: GetHandler-emits-singleton-resource (id=1 + RssSyncInterval=15 default), UpdateHandler-put-accepted-202 (round-trip + 4 setter calls counted via mutex-protected fake), UpdateHandler-rss-interval-zero-allowed (disable-sentinel pin), UpdateHandler-rss-interval-out-of-range-400 (RssSyncInterval=5 < 10), UpdateHandler-negative-rejected-400 (MinimumAge=-1), variable-aliases-non-nil, type-aliases compile-time pin. 3 new routes wired alongside the existing /api/v3/config/indexer routes in cmd/sonarr/host_wiring.go (re-uses configProv — the typed *config.Provider — same dep v3 uses). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go vet`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v5/indexersettings/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (1.3s + 3.7s). v5 surface coverage 32/~50 → 33/~50.

HEAD at `J279` — Wave 3 twenty-eighth slice: v5 **UpdateSettings** family port (51 LOC C# across UpdateSettingsController + UpdateSettingsResource + 55 LOC of shared SettingsController<T> base; effectively v5-NEW family — v3 has no UpdateConfigController equivalent). 3 endpoints: GET /api/v5/settings/update, GET /api/v5/settings/update/{id}, PUT /api/v5/settings/update/{id}. Status: GET → 200, PUT → 202 Accepted (matches SettingsController.SaveSettings TypedAccepted<TResource>). Resource has 4 wire fields: branch (string default "main"), updateAutomatically (bool default false), updateMechanism (camelCase enum: builtIn/script/external/apt/docker; persisted as PascalCase via apiv3.UpdateMechanismFromName roundtrip), updateScriptPath (string). Plus inherited `id` from RestResource (singleton — both GET endpoints emit id=1 by default or echo path-param id; PUT honours path id). Validation: SharedValidator chain ports the C# rule at UpdateSettingsController.cs:15-17 — UpdateScriptPath must be set + absolute path when UpdateMechanism == script; other mechanisms unrestricted. Invalid mechanism (not one of the 5 enum values) → 400 validation envelope citing UpdateMechanism via httperr.WriteValidation. DisallowUnknownFields rejects unknown body keys with 400. Persistence: writes via configfile.Provider's typed setters (SetBranch / SetUpdateAutomatically / SetUpdateMechanism / SetUpdateScriptPath) — all 4 fields live on IConfigFileProvider on C# so the v5 SettingsController.SaveSettings reflection-based SaveConfigDictionary pass is structurally equivalent to writing through the typed setters; the Go-port skips the reflection trip. The IConfigService.SaveConfigDictionary pass in C# is a no-op for this resource because none of the 4 fields are stored in the Configs table. New `internal/api/v5/updatesettings/` package (~180 LOC source + ~190 LOC test): local Provider interface (8 methods — 4 typed getters + 4 typed setters; *configfile.Provider satisfies it via wellknown.go) + Resource struct (4 wire fields + id) + GetHandler (shared between / and /{id} routes — defaults id=1, honours path-param when present) + UpdateHandler (decode + validate + UpdateMechanismFromName roundtrip + 4 sequential setter calls + re-read for response body) + private validate / write helpers. 8 tests: GetHandler-defaults-ID-1 (asserts branch=main, updateMechanism=builtIn camelCase), GetHandler-byID-honors-path-param (id=7 echoed), UpdateHandler-accepted-persists (script mechanism + absolute path → 202 + body round-trip + persisted UpdateMechanism="Script" PascalCase), UpdateHandler-script-mechanism-requires-absolute-path (400 on relative path), UpdateHandler-script-mechanism-requires-non-empty-path (400 on empty path with script), UpdateHandler-invalid-mechanism (400 validation envelope mentions UpdateMechanism), UpdateHandler-non-script-mechanism-path-not-validated (builtIn + empty path → 202; only script triggers validation), UpdateHandler-rejects-unknown-field (DisallowUnknownFields pin → 400). 3 new routes wired alongside the existing /api/v5/settings/importlist routes in cmd/sonarr/host_wiring.go (uses `cfg` — the boot-time *configfile.Provider — not `configProv` which is the typed *config.Provider for the unrelated Configs table). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go vet`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v5/updatesettings/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (1.3s + 3.7s). v5 surface coverage 31/~50 → 32/~50.

HEAD at `J278` — Wave 3 twenty-seventh slice: v5 **ReleaseProfile** family port (154 LOC C# across 2 files — ReleaseProfileController + ReleaseProfileResource under src/Sonarr.Api.V5/Profiles/Release/). 5 endpoints (1 GET list + 1 GET by-id + 1 POST + 1 PUT + 1 DELETE). TWO divergences from v3: (1) **Resource field rename + type change**: v3 emits `indexerId` (int — collapsed from `IndexerIDs.FirstOrDefault(0)` per `internal/api/v3/releaseprofile.go:109-114`); v5 emits `indexerIds` ([]int — full slice). Status codes match v3 directly on the other 4 endpoints: POST → 201 Created (v3 line 183 matches v5's `TypedCreated(model.Id)`), PUT → 202 Accepted (v3 line 215 matches `TypedAccepted(model.Id)`), GETs → 200. Required/Ignored on v3 C# are `object` polymorphic (accepts both string and List<string> for backward compat); v3 Go-port already pins to []string per resource.go:43-44, so v5's strict List<string> just locks in what's already on the wire. Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go vet`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v5/releaseprofile/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (1.3s + 3.7s). v5 surface coverage 30/~50 → 31/~50.

HEAD at `J276` — Wave 3 twenty-fifth slice: v5 **SystemBackup** family port (148 LOC C# across 2 files — BackupController + BackupResource under src/Sonarr.Api.V5/System/Backup/). 4 endpoints (1 GET + 1 DELETE + 2 POST). HEAD at `J275` — Wave 3 twenty-fourth slice: v5 **SystemTask** family port (79 LOC C# across 2 files — TaskController + TaskResource at src/Sonarr.Api.V5/System/Tasks/). 2 endpoints (GET list + GET by-id), both 200. ONE wire-shape divergence from v3 Go-port: v5 emits an additional `lastDuration` field (TimeSpan as string, computed as `LastExecution - LastStartTime` per TaskController.cs:56) which v3 Go-port omits entirely (J231 redacted the timing-derived field from the v3 corpus rather than emit it; v3 C# carries it as a computed property at TaskResource.cs:15 but Go-port chose absence). v5 surface MUST include it to match openapi schema. v5 wire shape: 8 fields total (id/name/taskName/interval/lastExecution/lastStartTime/nextExecution/lastDuration). `lastStartTime` shape difference: v5 openapi declares it non-nullable (`type: string, format: date-time`) whereas v3 Go-port uses `*time.Time` with `omitempty` — v5 emits unconditionally; when underlying Task.LastStartTime is nil, surface as zero `time.Time{}` which JSON-marshals to `"0001-01-01T00:00:00Z"` matching .NET default(DateTime) parity. `lastDuration` format: .NET TimeSpan default uses ToString("c") layout `[-][d.]hh:mm:ss[.fffffff]` — zero is `"00:00:00"`, five minutes is `"00:05:00"`, 1.5s is `"00:00:01.5000000"`. New `formatTimeSpan()` helper implements the canonical .NET TimeSpan rendering (handles days prefix + 7-digit ticks fractional). SignalR: v5 declares IHandle<CommandExecutedEvent> emitting ModelAction.Sync on the task channel — Wave 2 mop-up, not yet wired on either v3 or v5 Go-port side (same deferred state). New `internal/api/v5/systemtask/` package (155 LOC source + 161 LOC test): v5-specific Resource struct (8 fields) + Lister type alias to apiv3.SystemTaskLister + ListHandler + ByIDHandler + private taskToResource + private splitCamelCase (copy of v3 helper to avoid widening v3 exports for a single re-use) + private formatTimeSpan helper. 7 tests: ListHandler-empty-returns-array, ListHandler-populated-emits-lastDuration (pins ordering by Name + `"00:05:00"` format), ListHandler-lastStartTime-always-emitted (pins .NET default(DateTime) parity when LastStartTime nil), ByIDHandler-found, ByIDHandler-NotFound→404, ByIDHandler-invalid-id→400, NextExecution-contract (pins LastExecution + Interval-min), FormatTimeSpan-variants (sub-second 1.5s → `"00:00:01.5000000"` ticks). 2 new routes wired alongside existing /api/v3/system/task routes — re-uses taskSvc dep that already backs v3. Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go vet`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v5/systemtask/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (1.3s + 3.7s). v5 surface coverage 27/~50 → 28/~50.

Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go vet ./internal/api/v5/system/... ./cmd/sonarr/...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v5/system/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (7 v5/system tests + cmd/sonarr suite green, 1.4s + 3.7s respectively). v5 surface coverage 26/~50 → 27/~50. Both return 200. Pure path-alias at empty-corpus level (J251/J259/J264/J268 pattern). v3 Go-port already exposes both endpoints — GET emits `[]` from a stub handler per `internal/api/v3/manual_import.go:26-32` (production manual-import service pipeline — disk-walk + parser + decision-engine — isn't ported; same Wave 1 deferred chain as J258/J259/J261/J264/J268/J270); POST re-uses the existing reprocess logic that resolves series/episodes via ManualImportSeriesLookup + ManualImportEpisodeLookup. v5 ManualImportResource widens to 20 fields (path, relativePath, folderName, name, size, series, seasonNumber, episodes[], episodeFileId, releaseGroup, quality, languages[], qualityWeight, downloadId, customFormats[], customFormatScore, indexerFlags, releaseType, rejections[]) — invisible at empty-corpus where stub returns `[]`. v5 ManualImportReprocessResource POST body adds `relativePath` field — v3 POST uses `DisallowUnknownFields()` so a real v5 client sending relativePath would 400 (known follow-up tied to Wave 1 calculator + relative-path-fallback chain; empty-corpus replay never POSTs the field). New v5 GET query param `filterExistingFiles` (default true) ignored by v3 stub — invisible at empty-corpus. New `internal/api/v5/manualimport/` package: 3 type aliases (ReprocessItem = apiv3.ManualImportReprocessItem, SeriesLookup, EpisodeLookup; no Resource alias because v3 doesn't declare a per-row struct yet) + 2 variable aliases (GetHandler, ReprocessHandler). 5 tests: GET-empty-corpus-returns-200-empty-array with Content-Type pin, GET-invalid-seriesId-400, POST-empty-body-400 (items required), POST-malformed-body-400, variable-aliases non-nil + type-aliases compile-time pin. 2 new routes wired (re-uses seriesSvc + episodesSvc). Hard gates clean. v5 surface coverage 23/~50 → 24/~50.

HEAD at `J263` — Wave 3 twelfth slice: v5 **QualityDefinition** family (135 LOC C# source). Most complex Wave 3 family so far — THREE distinct wire-contract shifts in one family: (1) Resource shape REDUCED — v5 drops `minSize`/`maxSize`/`preferredSize` (v3 has 7 fields; v5 has 4: id/quality/title/weight); (2) Route map changed — v3 UpdateMany is at PUT `/qualitydefinition/update`, v5 puts it at PUT `/qualitydefinition` (no /update suffix); v3 has GET `/qualitydefinition/limits`, v5 removed that endpoint entirely; (3) Status code differs on UpdateMany — v3 emits 202+body, v5 emits 200+body. Because the Resource shape itself differs (drops 3 fields), all 4 handlers (List/ByID/Update/UpdateMany) are v5-specific — path-aliasing would emit the v3-with-size-fields shape. Custom v5 Resource + own QualityResource inline DTO (v3's `qualityResource` is package-private — duplicated 4 fields with explicit JSON tags rather than exporting v3's). Side-effect deferral: the C# v5 Update controller still calls `UpdateAllSizeLimits` when `model.MinSize/MaxSize/PreferredSize.HasValue`, but since the v5 wire Resource omits those fields, the deserialised model never has them set — branch is DEAD CODE in C# v5; Go-port v5 handlers skip the propagation entirely. 4 new routes wired (1 PUT goes to the bare /qualitydefinition path which conflicts with GET — Go router resolves by method). 8 tests: list-omits-size-fields (canonical reduction guard), ByID success + 404, Update success → 202, Update empty-title → 400, UpdateMany success → 200 (v5 demote from v3's 202), UpdateMany malformed-body → 400. Hard gates clean. v5 surface coverage 15/~50 → 16/~50.

HEAD at `J257` — Wave 3 sixth slice: v5 **CustomFilters** family (100 LOC C# source). First Wave 3 family port to USE the J256-consolidated `v5util.RewriteSuccessTo204` helper. Five v5 endpoints: GET list / GET by-id / POST / PUT /{id} / DELETE /{id}. Body shape identical to v3 (Resource alias — id / type / label / filters[]) per the openapi v5 schema; v5 uses `List<ExpandoObject>` for filters where v3 uses `List<JsonElement>`, both round-tripping the same wire bytes. Only status divergence: DELETE/{id} — v3 returns 200 + EMPTY body (J232 idempotency posture); v5 returns 204 NoContent + empty body. The expanded happy-body predicate from J256 (`{}` OR empty) is the motivating case for this slice — CustomFilter DELETE is the SOLE v3 handler intentionally emitting 200+empty for a non-`{}` happy path. New `internal/api/v5/customfilter/` package: 3 type aliases (Resource / Lister / Writer) + 4 variable aliases for the path-aliased handlers (List / ByID / Create / Update) + 1 wrapped handler (Delete via v5util.RewriteSuccessTo204). 4 tests: Delete success → 204 + empty + Content-Type-stripped + svc.Delete called once; Delete missing-id → 204 idempotent (J232 carry-through); variable-aliases-non-nil; type-aliases-match-v3 compile-time guard. 5 new routes wired alongside v3. HEAD at `J256` — Wave 1 consolidation slice closed: v5 response-rewriting middleware extracted from 3 duplicating packages (`importlist` / `metadata` / `seasonpass`) into a shared `internal/api/v5/internal/v5util/` package. Triggered by the J243-pattern four-duplication threshold — CustomFilters (next slice) needed the same wrapper, so the loop prompt's standing instruction fired the consolidation FIRST rather than create a fourth copy. New `v5util` package exports `BufferingWriter` (the http.ResponseWriter recorder) + 4 rewrite-helper variants matching all observed v3→v5 status-code patterns: `RewriteHappyTo204(sourceStatus, h)` (generic, takes source-status as param), `RewriteSuccessTo204(h)` shorthand for 200→204, `RewriteAcceptedTo204(h)` shorthand for 202→204, `RewriteAcceptedToOK(h)` for 202→200-with-body (the ImportList BulkUpdate case where v5 keeps the response array but demotes status). The happy-body predicate matches BOTH `{}` AND empty bodies — handles all three observed v3 emit shapes (200+`{}` from most provider DELETE/test handlers, 200+empty from CustomFilter DELETE, 202+`{}` from SeasonPass). Also exports `CopyHeaders`/`CopyHeadersExcept`. 8 unit tests on v5util covering the four wrapper variants + header preservation + Content-Type stripping. Three consumer packages collapsed: importlist.go drops ~110 LOC of inline middleware; metadata.go drops ~70 LOC; seasonpass.go drops ~85 LOC. Net: −~265 LOC of duplication, +~130 LOC of consolidated shared code with broader test coverage. Each consumer's call sites changed from `rewriteSuccessTo204(...)` style to `v5util.RewriteSuccessTo204(...)`. importlist_test.go had 4 inline middleware tests that pointed at the local helpers — deleted (covered by v5util tests now); unused `bytes` import dropped. Hard gates clean: go build ./..., go vet ./..., go test -race -count=1 on all v5 packages (10 packages green; no test failures across the v5 surface).

HEAD at `J255` — Wave 3 fifth slice: v5 **SeasonPass** family (99 LOC C# source). Single POST endpoint at `/api/v5/seasonpass`. Body shape identical to v3 (`Series[]` + nullable `MonitoringOptions`, per openapi v5 SeasonPassResource schema). Only divergence: success status code — v5 returns 204 NoContent + empty body via `TypedResults.NoContent()`; v3 returns 202 Accepted + body `{}`. Required a NEW wrapper variant `rewriteAcceptedTo204` (matches J253's `rewriteSuccessTo204` but detects 202 instead of 200) — the SeasonPass endpoint is the only v3 endpoint that uses 202 for a non-Created success, so the existing 200→204 wrapper doesn't fit. Type aliases for Resource / SeriesResource / SeasonOnWrite / MonitoringOptions / SeriesWriter / SeriesEmitter / EpisodeMonitorCascader keep the v5 namespace stable. Single new route wired via `seasonpassv5.PostHandler(seriesWriter, seriesEmitter, episodeMonitoredSvc)` — same deps the v3 route uses. 4 tests: success→204+empty+Content-Type-stripped+SignalR-Updated-fires+Monitored-mutation-landed (asserts the v3 cascade through-flows verbatim), malformed-body→400-envelope-passthrough (pins the wrapper-doesn't-corrupt-errors invariant per J253 pattern), missing-series→404-passthrough, type-aliases-match-v3 compile-time guard. HEAD at `J250` — Wave 1 deferred sub-arc closed: J237 `POST /api/v3/release/push` indexer + downloadclient name→id resolve. Two new narrow interfaces on the v3 package: `ReleasePushIndexerLookup` (Get + FindByName) and `ReleasePushDownloadClientLookup` (Get + FindByName); both satisfied by the existing `*indexers.Repo` and `*downloadclients.Repo` via Go structural typing (both already have `FindByName` from prior slices + `Get` via the embedded `repo.Repository[Foo]`). Trim whitespace on name input (C# `IsNullOrWhiteSpace` parity). On non-ErrNotFound DB errors → 400 with a generic "lookup failed" failure (the C# code throws which the ApiController converts to 4xx; Go-port uses the [Envelope] uniform shape per J233). Handler signature `ReleasePushHandler` grew from `(decisionMaker, dispatcher, cache)` to `(decisionMaker, dispatcher, cache, indexerLookup, dcLookup)` with both lookups nil-tolerant (when nil the resolver step skips entirely — matches the C# `if indexer == null` early-out branch which logs Debug and proceeds with body fields). host_wiring.go call site updated to pass `indexersRepo + downloadClientsRepo`. After successful resolve, `body.IndexerID + body.Indexer` get overwritten by the repo's canonical values before being baked into the synthetic `parser.ReleaseInfo` (C# `:97-98` mutation); `body.DownloadClientID + body.DownloadClient` similarly. 18 existing push-handler tests updated to pass `nil, nil` for the new params (no resolve behaviour exercised in those paths); 9 new tests cover the resolve: 5 end-to-end through the handler (by-id-only / by-name-only / id-not-found-400 / id-name-mismatch-400 / neither-supplied-null-return) + 4 unit tests on the helper directly (id-found-only-returns-byID / name-found-only-returns-byName / both-match-prefers-byID / name-not-found-validation). Hard gates clean: go build ./... + go vet ./... + go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v3/.

New `applyShouldOverride(ctx, body, cached, seriesLookup, episodeLookup)` helper handles the override path: validates the required body fields (SeriesId / EpisodeIds / Quality / Languages — matches C# `:86-90 Ensure.That` chain); looks up the series + episodes; decodes Quality (json.RawMessage → quality.Model via the type's own UnmarshalJSON) + Languages ([]language.Language); type-asserts the cached Subject to *decisionengine.Subject; shallow-clones the Subject + the nested ParsedEpisodeInfo (the cached entry is shared in-memory across concurrent requests so in-place mutation would corrupt it); overrides .Series / .Episodes / .ParsedEpisodeInfo.Quality / .Languages on the clone; swaps the Decision's Subject to the clone before dispatching. Preserves everything else verbatim from the cached Subject (Release / SceneMapping / MappedSeasonNumber / EpisodeRequested / DownloadAllowed / SeedConfiguration / CustomFormats / CustomFormatScore / SeriesMatchType / ReleaseSource) — matches the explicit field enumeration in C# `ReleaseController.cs:94-104` "new RemoteEpisode { ... }" clone. Handler signature grew by 2 params; host_wiring.go call site passes seriesSvc + episodesSvc which both already satisfy the narrow interfaces via Go structural typing. 5 existing tests updated to pass nil/nil deps (no override behaviour exercised); 4 new tests cover the override path: happy-path-overrides-and-dispatches (asserts seriesLookup got id=42; episodeLookup got [10,11]; dispatched Subject has overridden Series/Episodes/Languages/Quality; cached ParsedEpisodeInfo pointer NOT mutated in place — clone invariant), missing-required-fields-400 (table-driven across SeriesId/EpisodeIds/Quality/Languages absent), nil-deps-503, lookup-error-500. Hard gates clean: go build / go vet / go test -race on touched packages.

HEAD at `J247` — Cross-tool integration smoke test for the Phase 6.1 shadow-traffic triad landed at `cmd/shadowtraffic-e2e/` (~440 LOC across `doc.go` + `e2e_test.go`; the slice came in over the prompt's "~150 LOC" estimate because the subprocess lifecycle + port allocation + flush-timing scaffolding turned out denser than the test assertions). Both tests honour `-short` (skip on `go test -short ./...`) so the existing fast unit-test sweep isn't affected. Hard gates clean: go build ./..., go vet ./..., go test -race -count=1 ./cmd/shadowtraffic-e2e/... — under 5 seconds total wallclock. **The triad's format contract documented in J245 FORMAT.md is now end-to-end verified rather than asserted per-binary in isolation.** Closes slice (E) — the final autonomous-loop candidate. Autonomous-loop scope drained.

HEAD at `J245` — Shadow-traffic JSONL FORMAT.md landed at `cmd/shadow-traffic-record/FORMAT.md`. Authoritative operator-facing reference for the JSONL log format consumed by all three Phase 6.1 binaries (record writes; replay reads; diff doesn't touch it but chain-composes via replay → diff). Documents: per-field schema (`ts`/`method`/`path`/`headers`/`body`/`status` with their JSON tags + required/optional + meaning); body encoding contract (UTF-8 caveat for non-JSON; gzip in-place decompression per J244 with Content-Encoding stripped when decompression succeeded); single-value-per-key headers map convention; hop-by-hop + API-key + Content-Encoding redaction rules; Status=0 upstream-failure sentinel (and its `omitempty` interaction); `...<truncated>` body-truncation marker semantics (NOT replay-safe — it's an inspection knob); comments + blank lines (for hand-edited logs); examples for 7 entry shapes (plain GET / POST / originally-gzipped POST / API-key-stripped / upstream-unreachable / truncated / hand-edited-no-timestamp); inter-tool role matrix (record writes; replay reads; diff is a live proxy not a log consumer); stability + schema-evolution notes (additive optional fields are backward-compatible; mandatory-field additions break captures and need a version tag). Pure markdown; no Go code change. Closes slice (D). Hard gates clean (build + vet — nothing to test since no code changed).

HEAD at `J244` — Phase 6.1 shadow-traffic-record gains gzip request body decompression. Previously: a client POSTing `Content-Encoding: gzip` JSON resulted in the JSONL log storing the raw gzipped bytes — replay round-trips fine (it sends the same bytes upstream) but operator inspection saw garbage. Now: the recorder decompresses gzipped request bodies before writing them to the log, strips `Content-Encoding` from the captured Headers map (it would lie about the now-plain body), and crucially keeps the upstream-bound payload BYTE-IDENTICAL to what the client sent (semantic transparency — the proxy is invisible to the API contract). New `maybeDecompressForLog(body, encoding, limit)` helper in `writer.go` (~35 LOC): only matches `gzip` (case-insensitive); degrades to `(original, false)` on malformed input rather than panicking; bounded by a configurable decompression-bomb cap (defaults to writer's `maxBodyLen` if set, otherwise 8 MiB) — a few-KB gzip can expand to GiB so the limit is mandatory. `stripHopHeaders` gained a third parameter `dropContentEncoding bool`. `record()` calls `maybeDecompressForLog` and propagates the success flag to `stripHopHeaders`. 6 new tests + 1 test signature update (TestStripHopHeaders gained a third assertion column): helper-level (gzip happy / case-insensitive `gzip`/`GZIP`/`Gzip` / no-encoding + identity-encoding / malformed gzip / bomb-guard above-and-below limit) + end-to-end (gzipped POST decompressed in log + verbatim to upstream / plain POST recorded verbatim / malformed-gzip POST recorded verbatim with Content-Encoding header RETAINED — header is still truthful because the body in the log IS the bytes the client sent). Hard gates clean: go build ./... clean, go vet ./... clean, go test -race -count=1 ./cmd/shadow-traffic-record/... clean (28 tests, was 18 before the slice's 6 net new + 4 byte-eq sanity reinforcements).

HEAD at `J243` — Shared `cmd/internal/shadowtraffic/httputil` package landed. Dedups the byte-identical `singleJoin` + `isHopHeader` helpers that J239/J240/J241 each kept locally in `cmd/shadow-traffic-{diff,replay,record}/`. New package at `cmd/internal/shadowtraffic/httputil/` (intentional placement under `cmd/internal/` rather than the natural-sounding `cmd/shadowtraffic/internal/` because Go's `internal/` rule scopes import to the parent of `internal/`, and the three binaries are NOT under a hypothetical `cmd/shadowtraffic/...` subtree — they're siblings at `cmd/shadow-traffic-*`). Exports `SingleJoin(base, req string) string` + `IsHopHeader(h string) bool` with identical behaviour to the in-tree helpers. Each of the three binaries' `proxy.go` (and `record/writer.go` for `stripHopHeaders`) now imports `sonarr.tv/sonarr/cmd/internal/shadowtraffic/httputil` and calls `httputil.SingleJoin` / `httputil.IsHopHeader`. Local definitions deleted. Existing duplicate `TestSingleJoin` blocks in `diff/proxy_test.go` + `record/proxy_test.go` removed; a single consolidated test now lives at the shared package alongside an `IsHopHeader` test matrix (10 hop cases + 8 non-hop cases including the substring-trap case `X-Connection-Hint`). Net diff: +73 LOC shared package + ~-58 LOC across the three binaries → ~+15 LOC net (the helper tests are more comprehensive than the prior 4-case tables). Hard gates clean (go build ./..., go vet on all 4 touched packages, go test -race -count=1 on all 4 touched packages). The three binaries remain independently buildable; the only new symbol they share is the package import path.

HEAD at `J242` — Phase 6.3 nightly contract-suite CI workflow landed at `.github/workflows/nightly-contract.yml`. Runs on `cron: "0 3 * * *"` (03:00 UTC daily) + `workflow_dispatch` for manual debug. 4 jobs: **preflight** (gates on CONTRACT_STAGING_BASE_GO / CONTRACT_STAGING_BASE_CSHARP repo secrets — no-op when unset, typical fork case), **go-staging** (replays contract corpus against the Go-port staging deploy, gates on `CONTRACT_DRIFT_BASELINE=9` per the J217-era baseline established in build_go.yml; emits step-summary with drift count + baseline; uploads replay log artifact 14-day retention), **csharp-staging** (replays against the canonical C# staging — does NOT gate on baseline since C# IS the canonical; surfaces drift as a "golden corpus stale relative to live C#" signal for re-recording), **diff-summary** (cross-backend table with Go drift / C# drift / delta — clearly labels whether Go-port is at-or-ahead of C# for corpus-covered endpoints, or whether Go has N more drifting endpoints than C#). Uses the same `-run TestReplay -v | tee | grep "contract drift" || true` extraction pattern as the existing build_go.yml contract-replay job — known-working hack that surfaces per-entry drift count without making `go test` exit code drive the gate. The override_baseline workflow_dispatch input lets the operator one-shot a different baseline for debugging. Concurrency group prevents nightly overlap if a prior run is still draining (cancel-in-progress: false — finish what you started before launching the next). Phase 6.3 is "workflow YAML only, no code change" per PHASE_6_SCOPE.md and that's exactly what landed. Gated on secrets means this is **safe to merge today** even before staging URLs exist — preflight skips cleanly.

HEAD at `J241` — Phase 6.1 tee/recorder tool landed. New `cmd/shadow-traffic-record/` binary (~380 LOC source + ~360 LOC tests). Single-upstream reverse-proxy that tees inbound requests into a J240-compatible JSONL log file while forwarding each request to the configured backend. **Phase 6.1 toolkit triad now complete**: J241 captures → J240 replays → J239 dual-dispatches + diffs. Architecture: async LogWriter with bounded channel between request hot path and disk drain goroutine; non-blocking Enqueue with drop-counter when buffer full (best-effort capture under burst load — request latency never blocked on disk I/O); periodic buffered-writer flush (default 2s); optional per-request body length cap (default 256KiB) with `...<truncated>` marker for replay tooling; optional `-drop-api-keys` strips X-Api-Key + Authorization from captured records (default ON — captured logs are routinely emailed/Slack'd/checked-in, so default-deny on credential leakage); large response bodies streamed via io.Copy (not buffered); upstream-failure records with Status=0 sentinel; `-sync-on-close` fsync at shutdown. 14 tests including GET happy path (verifies path+query+timestamp+status), POST body verbatim capture (upstream echo assert), API key + Authorization stripped from log but PRESERVED upstream-bound (security boundary check), upstream-down → 502 to client + Status=0 record, hop-by-hop headers stripped (Connection drops, X-Custom preserved), queue-full drop-counter (100 entries → queue size 2 → some written + rest dropped + sum=100), body truncation with `<truncated>` suffix, Close idempotency + Enqueue-after-close returns false, append-mode (two open-write-close cycles → 2 entries in file), proxy stats increment, singleJoin + stripHopHeaders helpers, WriterStats.String, 1MB large-response streaming. Race-clean. Operator can now point Sonarr UI at this binary (`-listen :9001`, `-upstream http://localhost:8989`), drive normal workflow, get a JSONL log ready for J240 replay.

HEAD at `J240` — Phase 6.1 production-log replay helper landed. New `cmd/shadow-traffic-replay/` binary (~360 LOC source + ~440 LOC tests). Reads a JSONL request log streaming (4MiB max line — big POST bodies for manualimport / customformat-bulk fit); replays each entry against a target URL (typically the J239 shadow-traffic-diff listen addr). Pacing: `-speed 1.0` preserves recorded inter-entry timestamps, `0` no-throttle, `10.0` fast-forward; `-concurrency N` fans out across worker pool (disables timestamp pacing). Filters: `-method GET,POST` allowlist, `-path` prefix allowlist (CSV), `-status 200,4xx,503` (codes + classes). Per-request timeout via `-timeout`. Periodic stats lines + final summary (sent/filtered/errors/2xx/4xx/5xx/other counts + avg latency + rps). Atomic counters → race-clean. SIGINT/SIGTERM cancels the run mid-stream. Reads support comments (`#` lines) and blank lines for hand-edited logs. 18 tests covering log parse (happy, malformed, missing-required), filter (method/path/status/empty-passes-all), ParseStatusFilter (mixed codes+classes, bad-token, sub-100 reject), statusClassMatch (boundaries 199/200/299/300), replayer (dispatches each entry, preserves query string, respects filter, paces by timestamp at 2x speed within ±50ms tolerance, Speed=0 no-throttle near-instant, concurrency 5 actually parallelises, ctx cancel breaks long pacing wait, error counter on unreachable target, status bucket classification 2xx/4xx/5xx/other), OpenLogFile, splitCSV, StatsSnapshot.String. Hard gates clean. Together with J239 the operator has a complete Phase 6.1 toolkit: capture a request log somewhere (real Sonarr install, manual scripting), feed it to shadow-traffic-replay, point it at shadow-traffic-diff's listen addr, get a daily diff report.

HEAD at `J239` — Phase 6.1 shadow-traffic harness skeleton landed (Phase 6 prep begins). New `cmd/shadow-traffic-diff/` binary (~600 LOC source + ~370 LOC tests) accepts an incoming HTTP request stream, dispatches each request in parallel to two upstream backends (primary = canonical C# Sonarr, secondary = Go port), streams the primary's response back to the caller, then diffs the buffered secondary body against the primary post-redaction via the existing `internal/normalize` package. Daily diff reports written to disk grouped by severity (high/low) — high for status mismatch / missing field / type mismatch / secondary down, low for pure value diffs. OK events aggregated into a separate `*.ok-summary.json` counter so byte-equal requests don't pollute the per-event log. **Refactor**: moved `test/contract/internal/normalize/` → `internal/normalize/` so both the test/contract tree and the new `cmd/` binary can import it (was `internal` to `test/contract/`, blocking cmd/ imports). All 4 prior callsites updated. 14 tests including end-to-end through-front-proxy tests with both backends agreeing, status mismatch surfacing, value diffs as low-sev, missing fields as high-sev, secondary down as high-sev, primary down → 502 to client, POST body replayed verbatim to both backends, redacted-field-diff suppressed. Race-clean. Full build green. Existing contract suite green at the new normalize import path. Default `RedactConfig` covers the most common environmental noise (system/task lastDuration/nextExecution, command queued/started/ended/duration, log timestamps, system/status version/buildTime/runtimeVersion, diskspace freeSpace/totalSpace). Operator can override via `-redact-config` JSON file. Per the Phase 6 scope doc: this binary is the Phase 6.1 deliverable and can develop in parallel with staging-host provisioning — no infra needed yet.

HEAD at `J238` — Manualimport POST reprocess endpoint ported (audit row C.1.i-follow-5b closed). **All 5 J234-surfaced follow-up rows now closed. All originally-numbered C.1 + C.1.i-follow rows now closed. Phase 5 audit_fix_backlog fully drained — clear to pivot to Phase 6 prep.** New POST `/api/v3/manualimport` handler at `internal/api/v3/manual_import.go` mirrors C# `ManualImportController.ReprocessItems`. Body: array of `ManualImportReprocessItem`. Walks each item applying C# precedence: Series resolution prefers `Path` via narrow `ManualImportSeriesLookup` interface when `SeriesId<=0` and Path present (folder-based resolution mirroring C# `:113`); falls back to ID lookup. Episode resolution: explicit `EpisodeIDs` via narrow `ManualImportEpisodeLookup` interface takes precedence over parsed-info-derived stubs (C# `:51 processedItem.Episodes ?? new List<Episode>()`). Per-item `ReleaseType` recomputed via new `manualImportReleaseTypeFor(*parser.ParsedEpisodeInfo)` helper — `FullSeason → seasonPack`, `>=2 episodes → multiEpisode`, `==1 → singleEpisode`, else `unknown`. Wire format uses camelCase via `CamelCaseEnumName` helper (verified against `src/Sonarr.Api.V5/openapi.json` `ReleaseType` enum schema). Default-empty `CustomFormats` slice on nil to match C# null-as-empty JSON projection. Route wired via buildHostServer/v3Routes. 11 new tests at `manual_import_test.go` covering empty body, nil parsed-info → unknown, full-season → seasonPack, single-episode, multi-episode (E01-E03 spans two — the C# `:51 len >= 2` rule), explicit EpisodeIDs resolution, parsed-info fallback, Path-based series lookup, ID-based series lookup, CustomFormats nil → empty-slice normalisation. Hard gates clean. WRITE-side audit work — POST endpoint that mutates response shape per request body.
