- **J209** — EpisodeRefreshedService consumer: new `internal/episoderefreshed.Service` ports C# `Tv/EpisodeRefreshedService.cs` end-to-end. `Handle(EpisodeInfoRefreshedEvent)` subscriber filters Added (recently-aired + monitored, 14d/+1d window) + Updated (J207 AbsoluteEpisodeNumberAdded + same window) and buffers deduped IDs in a per-series cache. `Search(seriesID)` drains the cache, filters !HasFile via EpisodeLookup, dispatches via narrow EpisodeSearcher (nil-tolerant). AddOptions + series.Monitored gates honoured. Search call site (C# SeriesScannedHandler) DEFERRED — Go-port doesn't yet have a SeriesScannedEvent publisher; Search exposed as a public method for that future slice. Optional bus auto-subscribes Handle. 13 tests covering 8 Handle filter branches + 5 Search drain/dispatch paths.

New `episodeRefreshedSearcherAdapter` in `cmd/sonarr/main.go` wraps `*indexersearch.Service.EpisodeSearch` (per-ID `indexersearch.SearchSettings{}` call with warn-log absorb) into the slice-taking `EpisodeSearcher` shape — mirrors the loop+absorb pattern J39's `MissingEpisodeSearch` handler already uses internally. `episoderefreshed.NewService(bus, seriesSvc, episodesSvc, adapter, nil, logger)` constructed alongside the existing `refreshSeriesSvc` / `refreshEpisodesSvc` block. Handle auto-subscribes to the bus at boot.

The discarded `_, err =` from J210 became a named `episodeRefreshedSvc, err :=` so the subscriber closures can call its Search. - **J212** — UpdateEpisodeFileService SeriesScanned subscriber (C# `MediaFiles/UpdateEpisodeFileService.cs:103-136`, the `IHandle<SeriesScannedEvent>` arm). `episodefilemoving.UpdateService` grew `HandleSeriesScanned(ctx, ev)` method + 2 new narrow interfaces (`EpisodesWithFilesLister` + `EpisodeFileLookup`) + `SetSeriesScannedDeps` post-construction setter — keeps the slice-3.BK constructor signature stable. Decision tree line-aligned with C# `:105-135`: FileDate=None → no-op; `EpisodesWithFiles(seriesID)` → group by EpisodeFileID (so multi-episode files get a single mtime write) → per-group `GetFile` + `ChangeFileDateForFile` with warn-log-and-continue on per-file failures. Wired in `cmd/sonarr/main.go` next to the slice-3.BK constructor (line ~1973): `SetSeriesScannedDeps(episodesSvc, episodeFilesSvc)` + `messaging.Subscribe(bus, "episodefilemoving.HandleSeriesScanned", ...)`. 6 new tests: FileDateNone_NoOp / NoDepsWired_DebugLogsAndSkips / NilEvent_NoOp / GroupsByFileID_OneCallPerFile (two episodes → one fileID → one set call) / SkipsEpisodesWithNoFile (nil + zero-pointee both skipped) / ListerError_Propagates / PerFileLookupError_AbsorbedAndContinues.
- **J213** — `internal/episodemonitored.Service` port (C# `Tv/EpisodeMonitoredService.cs:14-247` modern path). `SetEpisodeMonitoredStatus(ctx, *series.Series, *MonitoringOptions) error` ports the 11 MonitorType branches plus Skip + nil-opts + Unknown early exits. Each branch toggles `episodes.Episode.Monitored` via a predicate matching C# `:53-130`. Post-toggle season cascade (C# `:132-169`) re-stamps `series.Seasons[].Monitored` with the 4 rules: last-season + (All OR (Future AND Continuing/Upcoming)) → true; first-season + Pilot → false; any-monitored-episode-in-season → true; else → false. Persists via `episodes.UpdateMany` + `series.UpdateSeries(opts.SkipPublishUpdatedEvent=true)` matching C# `publishUpdatedEvent: false`. Legacy v2 endpoint path at C# `:175-232` (Monitor==Unknown) DEFERRED with warn-log — requires `series.AddOptions` to grow the `IgnoreEpisodesWith[out]Files` fields first. 17 tests: 4 early-exit (nil-opts/Unknown/Skip/nil-series) + 11 branch (one per MonitorType) + 4 cascade rules. Not yet wired into any consumer; the seasonpass.go deferred cascade + the SeriesScannedHandler post-add-actions arm are the two natural follow-up wirers.

Wired at `host_wiring.go` next to the SeriesWriter construction with `episodemonitored.NewService(episodesSvc, seriesSvc, nil, logger)` constructed inline (no buildHostServer signature churn — episodesSvc + seriesSvc were already in scope). 3 new tests: CascaderWired_RoutesThroughCascader (cascader invoked with correct MonitorType) / CascaderWired_NoMonitoringOptions_FallsBackToUpdate (no MonitoringOptions → fallback path) / CascaderError_Propagates500 (cascader err → 500 not silent swallow). 7 existing tests updated to pass nil cascader.
- **J242** — Phase 6.3 nightly contract-suite CI workflow (`.github/workflows/nightly-contract.yml`). 4 jobs: preflight (skips when CONTRACT_STAGING_BASE_GO/CSHARP secrets unset — fork-safe), go-staging (gates on CONTRACT_DRIFT_BASELINE=9, fails workflow on new drift, step-summary + 14-day artifact retention), csharp-staging (no baseline gate, surfaces "corpus stale relative to live C#" signal for re-recording), diff-summary (Go drift vs C# drift cross-backend table). Runs on cron "0 3 * * *" + workflow_dispatch with override_baseline input. Uses the same drift-count extraction pattern as build_go.yml's existing contract-replay job — known-working. Concurrency group prevents nightly overlap. Phase 6.3 is "workflow YAML only" per PHASE_6_SCOPE.md and that's exactly what landed.

- **J241** — Phase 6.1 tee/recorder tool (`cmd/shadow-traffic-record/`). Single-upstream reverse-proxy that tees inbound traffic into a J240-compatible JSONL log file while forwarding each request to the backend. Async LogWriter with bounded queue + drop-counter (non-blocking Enqueue — best-effort under burst load); periodic flush (2s default); per-request body cap (256KiB default) with `...<truncated>` marker; `-drop-api-keys` default ON strips X-Api-Key + Authorization from log but preserves them upstream-bound; large bodies io.Copy-streamed (not buffered); upstream-failure records with Status=0; `-sync-on-close` fsync. 14 tests including GET happy path, POST body verbatim, API-key stripped from log but kept upstream-bound, upstream-down → 502 + Status=0 record, hop-by-hop headers stripped, queue-full drop-counter, body truncation marker, Close idempotency, append-mode across two open-write-close cycles, large-response streaming (1MB). Race-clean. **Phase 6.1 toolkit triad now complete**: J241 capture → J240 replay → J239 diff.

- **J240** — Phase 6.1 production-log replay helper (`cmd/shadow-traffic-replay/`). Companion binary to J239's harness. Reads JSONL request logs streaming (4MiB line max for big POST bodies); replays against a target URL. Pacing: `-speed N` multiplier (1.0=real-time, 0=no-throttle, 10.0=fast-forward) preserves recorded inter-entry timestamps via prev-Timestamp gap; `-concurrency N` worker pool (disables timestamp pacing for serial-order destruction). Filters by method (CSV), path-prefix (CSV), status (codes + classes like "4xx" mixed). Per-request timeout, periodic stats lines + final summary, SIGINT cancels mid-run. Comments (`#`) and blank lines in logs supported. 18 tests including timestamp-paced 2x replay within ±50ms tolerance, concurrent dispatch (max-in-flight assertion), ctx cancel mid-pacing-wait, error counter on dead target, status bucket classification. Race-clean. Together with J239 the operator has a Phase 6.1 toolkit complete — capture log → shadow-traffic-replay → shadow-traffic-diff listen → daily diff report.

- **J239** — Phase 6.1 shadow-traffic harness skeleton (`cmd/shadow-traffic-diff/`). Reverse-proxy binary that dispatches each incoming request to C# + Go backends in parallel, returns primary's response, diffs secondary's body post-redaction via `internal/normalize`, writes daily diff reports grouped by severity (high/low) + OK aggregate. **Refactor**: moved `test/contract/internal/normalize/` → `internal/normalize/` (4 import sites updated) so cmd/ can import it. 14 tests including end-to-end-through-front-proxy paths covering byte-equal-no-diff, status mismatch HIGH, value diff LOW, missing field HIGH, secondary-down HIGH, primary-down 502-to-client, POST body replayed verbatim, redacted-field-diff suppressed. Race-clean. Full build green. Existing contract suite still passes at the new normalize path. Default RedactConfig covers system/task lastDuration, command queued/started/ended, log timestamps, system/status build fields, diskspace free/total. Operator overrides via `-redact-config` JSON. **Phase 5 → Phase 6 pivot complete.** Phase 6 entrance is NOT gated on this — it can develop in parallel with staging-host provisioning. The smoke runbook (Phase 6.2) is operator-driven and not autonomous-loop work; the nightly contract-suite CI (Phase 6.3) is a workflow file change not a code change.

Two narrow interfaces (`ManualImportSeriesLookup` for folder-path-based series resolution, `ManualImportEpisodeLookup` for explicit-EpisodeIDs path). New `manualImportReleaseTypeFor` helper maps `*parser.ParsedEpisodeInfo` shape to wire-format ReleaseType string (`seasonPack`/`multiEpisode`/`singleEpisode`/`unknown`) via `CamelCaseEnumName` (matches C# `src/Sonarr.Api.V5/openapi.json` `ReleaseType` enum schema). Default-empty CustomFormats slice on nil to match C# null-as-empty JSON projection. Route wired via buildHostServer/v3Routes. 11 new tests covering empty-body / nil-parsed-info / season-pack / single-episode / multi-episode (E01-E03 → spans 2 episodes → multiEpisode per C# `:51` len-rule) / explicit-EpisodeIDs / parsed-info-fallback / Path-based-series / ID-based-series / CustomFormats-nil-empty-slice. **All 5 J234-surfaced follow-up rows now closed. - **J236** — Queue/grab POST endpoints ported (audit row C.1.i-follow-5c closed). New `internal/api/v3/queue_grab.go` ports POST /api/v3/queue/grab/{id} + POST /api/v3/queue/grab/bulk matching C# `QueueActionController.Grab`. Per-id pipeline: pending lookup → JSON deserialise to `parser.ReleaseInfo` + `parser.ParsedEpisodeInfo` → series resolve → minimal `*decisionengine.Subject` (with `DownloadAllowed=true` to clear isQualified) → `ProcessDecisions` dispatch. 404 short-circuits bulk on first miss; malformed-body 400; empty-ids 200 no-dispatch (matches C# zero-iteration semantics). 3 narrow interfaces defined in v3 package (PendingReleaseLookup, QueueGrabSeriesLookup, QueueGrabDispatcher) — each satisfied by existing services via Go structural typing. - **J233** — Validation envelope policy (audit row C.1.i-follow-2 closed). Documented C#'s 4 envelope shapes from J228 reference + per-path decisions. Go-port deliberately keeps single [Envelope] shape across all 4xx/5xx rather than mirroring C#'s framework-incidental mix (RFC 9110 problem-details on `[ApiController]` paths + legacy `{message, content}` on bulk-empty-ids + null-deref-bug 500s). Rationale: consistent single parser handles every Go-port error response; C#'s mix is ASP.NET Core defaults, not deliberate. Pinned via `internal/httperr/httperr.go` package doc-comment block + new `TestPolicy_DoesNotEmitRFC9110ProblemDetails` regression-guard test covering BadRequest/NotFound/Conflict. WRITE+READ side (404 read envelopes + 4xx write envelopes both covered). No production code change. Hard gates clean. 1 of 5 follow-up rows remains.

Special cases retained: delayprofile id==1 still 405, qualityprofile ErrQualityProfileInUse still 409. 12 v3 tests renamed `*_NotFound404` → `*_Idempotent`; +6 emit-suppression assertions. 1 v5/importlist test renamed (v3 200 → v5 204 via rewriteSuccessTo204). WRITE-side audit work — DELETE is state-changing; the GET-not-found read-side untouched. Hard gates clean. 2 of 5 follow-up rows remain.

Fourth standalone C.1 closure this loop-run.

- **J219** — Pushover end-to-end encryption port (audit row C.1.f closed; upstream commit `e79077d5f`). New `EncryptionKey string` field on `pushover.Settings` mirroring C# `PushoverSettings.cs:57`. New `pushover/encryption.go` ports the C# `EncryptField` / `GzipCompress` / `HexStringToBytes` / `validateEncryptionKey` helpers from `PushoverProxy.cs:97-156`: AES-256-CBC + PKCS#7 padding + random IV + HMAC-SHA256 authentication + gzip compression, base64-encoded envelope. `sendNotification` modified to encrypt title + message when EncryptionKey is set + add `encrypted=1` to the form. Plaintext path preserved when key is empty. Settings validator added with the same regex (`^[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$`) C# uses. 14 new tests: 4 settings-validator branches (empty / valid / 4 invalid patterns) + helper tests for hexStringToBytes / pkcs7Pad full-block + partial-block padding + critical EncryptField_RoundTripsCleanly (5 plaintexts: ASCII / empty / 1KB-compressible / multi-line / UTF-8) that inverts the envelope via the same crypto primitives Pushover's client uses + IV uniqueness test + wrong-key-length error + 2 integration tests through OnGrab (encrypted-flag + ciphertext when key set; plaintext wire when key absent) + 1 settings-integration test for the EncryptionKey rejection path.
- **J218** — Audit row C.1.h CLOSED (upstream commit `5477cd262` — "Add CancellationToken to HttpClient async methods"). Pure verification slice: the C# upstream added `CancellationToken` params to the entire `IHttpClient` async surface; the Go-port already uses `context.Context` end-to-end via `http.NewRequestWithContext` at the dispatcher seam (`httpclient/dispatcher.go:233`). Whole-tree grep for `http.NewRequest` outside of `WithContext` returns zero matches. No production-code change required — the Go-port's architecture was already aligned from day 1. Added `TestDispatcher_ContextCancellation_PropagatesToHTTPClient` to `httpclient/dispatcher_test.go` as a durable regression guard: server delays response, client cancels via ctx, Dispatch must return `context.Canceled`. A future refactor that swaps `NewRequestWithContext` for `NewRequest` will fail this test.
- **J217** — Forward-port of upstream C# commit `b81de8570` (audit row C.1.j — "Recently-aired refresh logging"). Adds the info-log line at C# `EpisodeRefreshedService.cs:47` ("Searching for {MissingCount} episodes from '{SeriesTitle}' that were recently added or had absolute episode number added") to the Go-port's `episoderefreshed.Service.Search`. C# upstream changed the Search signature from `Search(int seriesId)` to `Search(Series series)` so the new log could carry the series title — Go-port preserves the by-id signature and loads the series via the existing SeriesLookup dep internally. Series-lookup error absorption added: a GetSeries failure on the title fetch degrades to a less-informative log line + the dispatch still runs (matches the established warn-and-continue posture from J191). Independent of C.1.a v5 merge — one self-contained behaviour ported standalone. 1 new test: `Search_SeriesLookupErr_AbsorbedDispatchStillRuns` verifies the absorption + dispatch still fires.
- **J216** — SeriesScannedHandler post-add-actions arm port (C# `Tv/SeriesScannedHandler.cs:36-74` AddOptions!=null branch — the partner to the J211 AddOptions==null partial port). New `internal/seriesadd.Service` with `HandleSeriesScanned` + `HandleSeriesScanSkipped` subscribers running the 4-step C# sequence: (1) `episodemonitored.Service.SetEpisodeMonitoredStatus` cascade per AddOptions.Monitor; (2) publish new `series.SeriesAddCompletedEvent`; (3) push search command based on AddOptions search flags (`SeriesSearch` when both flags / `MissingEpisodeSearch` when only-Missing / DEFERRED when only-CutoffUnmet since handler not yet ported); (4) `series.Service.RemoveAddOptions` to clear the post-add gate. All step errors absorbed via warn-log + continue so the post-add lifecycle completes as far as possible. 4 narrow interfaces (MonitorCascader / CommandPusher / AddOptionsClearer + optional bus); each subscriber gates the opposite way on AddOptions from the J211 episoderefreshed subscribers, so the two paths never double-handle. Wired at main.go next to the J211 subscribers; constructs a separate episodemonitored.Service instance for the cascade (the J214 instance lives in host_wiring.go scope). 14 tests covering: 3 constructor nil-dep validation + 3 AddOptions gate paths (nil-series / no-AddOptions / malformed-AddOptions) + 4 search-flag branches (both / only-missing / only-cutoff-deferred / neither) + 3 error-absorption tests + 1 scan-skipped routing test + 1 SeriesAddCompletedEvent publish test (uses real messaging.Bus with subscriber). **The full SeriesScannedHandler is now ported end-to-end (J211 + J216 cover both AddOptions branches).**

- **J215** — `SeriesSearchCommand` handler port (C# `IndexerSearch/SeriesSearchService.cs:33-75`). New `internal/commands/handlers/seriessearch.go` with `Execute(ctx, body) error` dispatching on two branches: no monitored seasons → per-episode mode (filter monitored+missing+aired, per-episode dispatch); ≥1 monitored season → per-season mode (sort by SeasonNumber ASC, skip unmonitored, per-season dispatch). 4 narrow optional interfaces (SeriesSearchLookup, SeriesSearchEpisodesLister, EpisodeSearcher reused from J39, SeasonSearcher new). Documented divergences: quality-profile `UpgradeAllowed` → `missingOnly` hard-coded false (matches C# default profile); Grabbed-count tracking deferred (matches J39 — counts decisions). Wired into commands/handlers/Deps + RegisterAll; main.go gets new `seasonSearchAdapter` wrapping `*indexersearch.Service.SeasonSearch` into the decisions-count shape, alongside the existing per-episode `handlerEpisodeSearchAdapter` (J39). 9 tests covering nil-deps stub + body validation + series-not-found + per-episode mode filter predicate (5 edge cases) + per-season ASC ordering + skip-unmonitored + per-episode error absorb + per-season error absorb + empty-Seasons-fallthrough-to-per-episode. Closes the second dependency the SeriesScannedHandler post-add-actions arm needs (after J213 closed EpisodeMonitoredService); remaining: SeriesAddCompletedEvent type definition.
